000000r 1               .macpack longbranch
000000r 2               .macro  jeq     Target
000000r 2                       .if     .match(Target, 0)
000000r 2                       bne     *+5
000000r 2                       jmp     Target
000000r 2                       .elseif .def(Target) .and .const((*-2)-(Target)) .and ((*+2)-(Target) <= 127)
000000r 2                               beq     Target
000000r 2                       .else
000000r 2                               bne     *+5
000000r 2                               jmp     Target
000000r 2                       .endif
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               .macro  jne     Target
000000r 2                       .if     .match(Target, 0)
000000r 2                               beq     *+5
000000r 2                               jmp     Target
000000r 2                       .elseif .def(Target) .and .const((*-2)-(Target)) .and ((*+2)-(Target) <= 127)
000000r 2                               bne     Target
000000r 2                       .else
000000r 2                               beq     *+5
000000r 2                               jmp     Target
000000r 2                       .endif
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               .macro  jmi     Target
000000r 2                       .if     .match(Target, 0)
000000r 2                               bpl     *+5
000000r 2                               jmp     Target
000000r 2                       .elseif .def(Target) .and .const((*-2)-(Target)) .and ((*+2)-(Target) <= 127)
000000r 2                               bmi     Target
000000r 2                       .else
000000r 2                               bpl     *+5
000000r 2                               jmp     Target
000000r 2                       .endif
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               .macro  jpl     Target
000000r 2                       .if     .match(Target, 0)
000000r 2                               bmi     *+5
000000r 2                               jmp     Target
000000r 2                       .elseif .def(Target) .and .const((*-2)-(Target)) .and ((*+2)-(Target) <= 127)
000000r 2                               bpl     Target
000000r 2                       .else
000000r 2                               bmi     *+5
000000r 2                               jmp     Target
000000r 2                       .endif
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               .macro  jcs     Target
000000r 2                       .if     .match(Target, 0)
000000r 2                               bcc     *+5
000000r 2                               jmp     Target
000000r 2                       .elseif .def(Target) .and .const((*-2)-(Target)) .and ((*+2)-(Target) <= 127)
000000r 2                               bcs     Target
000000r 2                       .else
000000r 2                               bcc     *+5
000000r 2                               jmp     Target
000000r 2                       .endif
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               .macro  jcc     Target
000000r 2                       .if     .match(Target, 0)
000000r 2                               bcs     *+5
000000r 2                               jmp     Target
000000r 2                       .elseif .def(Target) .and .const((*-2)-(Target)) .and ((*+2)-(Target) <= 127)
000000r 2                               bcc     Target
000000r 2                       .else
000000r 2                               bcs     *+5
000000r 2                               jmp     Target
000000r 2                       .endif
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               .macro  jvs     Target
000000r 2                       .if     .match(Target, 0)
000000r 2                               bvc     *+5
000000r 2                               jmp     Target
000000r 2                       .elseif .def(Target) .and .const((*-2)-(Target)) .and ((*+2)-(Target) <= 127)
000000r 2                               bvs     Target
000000r 2                       .else
000000r 2                               bvc     *+5
000000r 2                               jmp     Target
000000r 2                       .endif
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               .macro  jvc     Target
000000r 2                       .if     .match(Target, 0)
000000r 2                               bvs     *+5
000000r 2                               jmp     Target
000000r 2                       .elseif .def(Target) .and .const((*-2)-(Target)) .and ((*+2)-(Target) <= 127)
000000r 2                               bvc     Target
000000r 2                       .else
000000r 2                               bvs     *+5
000000r 2                               jmp     Target
000000r 2                       .endif
000000r 2               .endmacro

000000r 1               .include "macros.s"     ; reviewed
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               ;  sigh!  Some truly genius macro magic here
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               ; htasc - set the hi bit on the last byte of a string for termination
000000r 2               ; (by Tom Greene)
000000r 2               .macro htasc str
000000r 2               	.repeat	.strlen(str)-1,I
000000r 2               		.byte	.strat(str,I)
000000r 2               	.endrep
000000r 2               	.byte	.strat(str,.strlen(str)-1) | $80
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; For every token, a byte gets put into segment "DUMMY".
000000r 2               ; This way, we count up with every token. The DUMMY segment
000000r 2               ; doesn't get linked into the binary.
000000r 2               .macro init_token_tables
000000r 2                       .segment "VECTORS"
000000r 2               TOKEN_ADDRESS_TABLE:
000000r 2                       .segment "KEYWORDS"
000000r 2               TOKEN_NAME_TABLE:
000000r 2               		.segment "DUMMY"
000000r 2               DUMMY_START:
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; optionally define token symbol
000000r 2               ; count up token number
000000r 2               .macro define_token token
000000r 2                       .segment "DUMMY"
000000r 2               		.ifnblank token
000000r 2               			token := <(*-DUMMY_START)+$80
000000r 2               		.endif
000000r 2               		.res 1; count up in any case
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; lay down a keyword, optionally define a token symbol
000000r 2               .macro keyword key, token
000000r 2               		.segment "KEYWORDS"
000000r 2               		htasc	key
000000r 2               		define_token token
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; lay down a keyword and an address (RTS style),
000000r 2               ; optionally define a token symbol
000000r 2               .macro keyword_rts key, vec, token
000000r 2                   .segment "VECTORS"
000000r 2               		  .word	vec-1
000000r 2               		  keyword key, token
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; lay down a keyword and an address,
000000r 2               ; optionally define a token symbol
000000r 2               .macro keyword_addr key, vec, token
000000r 2                   .segment "VECTORS"
000000r 2               		  .addr	vec
000000r 2               		  keyword key, token
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro count_tokens
000000r 2                   .segment "DUMMY"
000000r 2               		   NUM_TOKENS := <(*-DUMMY_START)
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro init_error_table
000000r 2                       .segment "ERROR"
000000r 2               ERROR_MESSAGES:
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro define_error error, msg
000000r 2                   .segment "ERROR"
000000r 2               		   error := <(*-ERROR_MESSAGES)
000000r 2               		   htasc msg
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ;---------------------------------------------
000000r 2               ; set the MSB of every byte of a string
000000r 2               .macro asc80 str
000000r 2               	.repeat	.strlen(str),I
000000r 2               		.byte	.strat(str,I)+$80
000000r 2               	.endrep
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2

000000r 2               ; added for HYDRA-16
000000r 2               ;----------------------------------------------
000000r 2               .macro  WRITE_SEQ  C1, C2, C3, C4, C5, C6, C7, C8, C9
000000r 2               .ifblank   C1
000000r 2                   .exitmacro
000000r 2               .else
000000r 2                          lda             C1
000000r 2                          jsr             MONCOUT
000000r 2               .endif
000000r 2                          WRITE_SEQ  C2, C3, C4, C5, C6, C7, C8, C9
000000r 2               .endmacro

000018r 1               .include "memory.s"       ; reviewed
000018r 2               ; generic stack and memory management code
000018r 2               ; this code is identical across all versions of
000018r 2               ; BASIC
000018r 2               
000018r 2               .segment "CODE"
000018r 2               
000018r 2               ; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
000018r 2               ; CALLED BY "NEXT" AND "FOR" TO SCAN THROUGH
000018r 2               ; THE STACK FOR A FRAME WITH THE SAME VARIABLE.
000018r 2               ;
000018r 2               ; (FORPNT) = ADDRESS OF VARIABLE IF "FOR" OR "NEXT"
000018r 2               ; 	= $XXFF IF CALLED FROM "RETURN"
000018r 2               ; 	<<< BUG: SHOULD BE $FFXX >>>
000018r 2               ;
000018r 2               ;	RETURNS .NE. IF VARIABLE NOT FOUND,
000018r 2               ;	(X) = STACK PNTR AFTER SKIPPING ALL FRAMES
000018r 2               ;
000018r 2               ;	.EQ. IF FOUND
000018r 2               ;	(X) = STACK PNTR OF FRAME FOUND
000018r 2               ; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
000018r 2               GTFORPNT:
000018r 2  BA                   tsx
000019r 2  E8                   inx
00001Ar 2  E8                   inx
00001Br 2  E8                   inx
00001Cr 2  E8                   inx
00001Dr 2               L2279:
00001Dr 2  BD 01 01             lda     STACK+1,x
000020r 2  C9 81                cmp     #$81
000022r 2  D0 21                bne     L22A1
000024r 2  A5 BA                lda     FORPNT+1
000026r 2  D0 0A                bne     L228E
000028r 2  BD 02 01             lda     STACK+2,x
00002Br 2  85 B9                sta     FORPNT
00002Dr 2  BD 03 01             lda     STACK+3,x
000030r 2  85 BA                sta     FORPNT+1
000032r 2               L228E:
000032r 2  DD 03 01             cmp     STACK+3,x
000035r 2  D0 07                bne     L229A
000037r 2  A5 B9                lda     FORPNT
000039r 2  DD 02 01             cmp     STACK+2,x
00003Cr 2  F0 07                beq     L22A1
00003Er 2               L229A:
00003Er 2  8A                   txa
00003Fr 2  18                   clc
000040r 2  69 12                adc     #BYTES_PER_FRAME
000042r 2  AA                   tax
000043r 2  D0 D8                bne     L2279
000045r 2               L22A1:
000045r 2  60                   rts
000046r 2               
000046r 2               ; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
000046r 2               ; MOVE BLOCK OF MEMORY UP
000046r 2               ;
000046r 2               ; ON ENTRY:
000046r 2               ;	(Y,A) = (HIGHDS) = DESTINATION END+1
000046r 2               ;	(LOWTR) = LOWEST ADDRESS OF SOURCE
000046r 2               ;	(HIGHTR) = HIGHEST SOURCE ADDRESS+1
000046r 2               ; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
000046r 2               BLTU:
000046r 2  20 rr rr             jsr     REASON
000049r 2  85 A1                sta     STREND
00004Br 2  84 A2                sty     STREND+1
00004Dr 2               BLTU2:
00004Dr 2  38                   sec
00004Er 2  A5 CA                lda     HIGHTR
000050r 2  E5 CF                sbc     LOWTR
000052r 2  85 92                sta     INDEX
000054r 2  A8                   tay
000055r 2  A5 CB                lda     HIGHTR+1
000057r 2  E5 D0                sbc     LOWTR+1
000059r 2  AA                   tax
00005Ar 2  E8                   inx
00005Br 2  98                   tya
00005Cr 2  F0 23                beq     L22DD
00005Er 2  A5 CA                lda     HIGHTR
000060r 2  38                   sec
000061r 2  E5 92                sbc     INDEX
000063r 2  85 CA                sta     HIGHTR
000065r 2  B0 03                bcs     L22C6
000067r 2  C6 CB                dec     HIGHTR+1
000069r 2  38                   sec
00006Ar 2               L22C6:
00006Ar 2  A5 C8                lda     HIGHDS
00006Cr 2  E5 92                sbc     INDEX
00006Er 2  85 C8                sta     HIGHDS
000070r 2  B0 08                bcs     L22D6
000072r 2  C6 C9                dec     HIGHDS+1
000074r 2  90 04                bcc     L22D6
000076r 2               L22D2:
000076r 2  B1 CA                lda     (HIGHTR),y
000078r 2  91 C8                sta     (HIGHDS),y
00007Ar 2               L22D6:
00007Ar 2  88                   dey
00007Br 2  D0 F9                bne     L22D2
00007Dr 2  B1 CA                lda     (HIGHTR),y
00007Fr 2  91 C8                sta     (HIGHDS),y
000081r 2               L22DD:
000081r 2  C6 CB                dec     HIGHTR+1
000083r 2  C6 C9                dec     HIGHDS+1
000085r 2  CA                   dex
000086r 2  D0 F2                bne     L22D6
000088r 2  60                   rts
000089r 2               
000089r 2               ; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
000089r 2               ; CHECK IF ENOUGH ROOM LEFT ON STACK
000089r 2               ; FOR "FOR", "GOSUB", OR EXPRESSION EVALUATION
000089r 2               ; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
000089r 2               CHKMEM:
000089r 2  0A                   asl     a
00008Ar 2  69 3E                adc     #SPACE_FOR_GOSUB
00008Cr 2  B0 35                bcs     MEMERR
00008Er 2  85 92                sta     INDEX
000090r 2  BA                   tsx
000091r 2  E4 92                cpx     INDEX
000093r 2  90 2E                bcc     MEMERR
000095r 2  60                   rts
000096r 2               
000096r 2               ; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
000096r 2               ; CHECK IF ENOUGH ROOM BETWEEN ARRAYS AND STRINGS
000096r 2               ; (Y,A) = ADDR ARRAYS NEED TO GROW TO
000096r 2               ; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
000096r 2               REASON:
000096r 2  C4 A4                cpy     FRETOP+1
000098r 2  90 28                bcc     L231E
00009Ar 2  D0 04                bne     L22FC
00009Cr 2  C5 A3                cmp     FRETOP
00009Er 2  90 22                bcc     L231E
0000A0r 2               L22FC:
0000A0r 2  48                   pha
0000A1r 2  A2 09                ldx     #FAC-TEMP1-1
0000A3r 2  98                   tya
0000A4r 2               L2300:
0000A4r 2  48                   pha
0000A5r 2  B5 C7                lda     TEMP1,x
0000A7r 2  CA                   dex
0000A8r 2  10 FA                bpl     L2300
0000AAr 2  20 rr rr             jsr     GARBAG
0000ADr 2  A2 F7                ldx     #TEMP1-FAC+1
0000AFr 2               L230B:
0000AFr 2  68                   pla
0000B0r 2  95 D1                sta     FAC,x
0000B2r 2  E8                   inx
0000B3r 2  30 FA                bmi     L230B
0000B5r 2  68                   pla
0000B6r 2  A8                   tay
0000B7r 2  68                   pla
0000B8r 2  C4 A4                cpy     FRETOP+1
0000BAr 2  90 06                bcc     L231E
0000BCr 2  D0 05                bne     MEMERR
0000BEr 2  C5 A3                cmp     FRETOP
0000C0r 2  B0 01                bcs     MEMERR
0000C2r 2               L231E:
0000C2r 2  60                   rts

0000C5r 2               ;

0000DFr 2  A0 rr                ldy     #>QT_ERROR

0000E1r 2               ; PRINT STRING AT (Y,A)

0003FBr 2               ;  PGS 2/13/26 - removed most conditionals for old machine versions
0003FBr 2               ;
0003FBr 2               ; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
0003FBr 2               ; "FOR" STATEMENT
0003FBr 2               ;
0003FBr 2               ; FOR PUSHES 18 BYTES ON THE STACK:
0003FBr 2               ; 2 -- TXTPTR
0003FBr 2               ; 2 -- LINE NUMBER
0003FBr 2               ; 5 -- INITIAL (CURRENT)  FOR VARIABLE VALUE
0003FBr 2               ; 1 -- STEP SIGN
0003FBr 2               ; 5 -- STEP VALUE
0003FBr 2               ; 2 -- ADDRESS OF FOR VARIABLE IN VARTAB
0003FBr 2               ; 1 -- FOR TOKEN ($81)
0003FBr 2               ; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
0003FBr 2               FOR:
0003FBr 2  A9 80                lda     #$80
0003FDr 2  85 81                sta     SUBFLG
0003FFr 2  20 rr rr             jsr     LET
000402r 2  20 rr rr             jsr     GTFORPNT
000405r 2  D0 05                bne     L2619

00040Br 2  9A                   txs

00040Dr 2  68                   pla

000413r 2  20 rr rr             jsr     DATAN
000416r 2  18                   clc
000417r 2  98                   tya

000651r 2               ;
000651r 2               ; CONVERT LINE NUMBER
000651r 2               ; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
000651r 2               LINGET:
000651r 2  A2 00                ldx     #$00
000653r 2  86 48                stx     LINNUM
000655r 2  86 49                stx     LINNUM+1
000657r 2               L28BE:
000657r 2  B0 F7                bcs     L28B7
000659r 2  E9 2F                sbc     #$2F
00065Br 2  85 7A                sta     CHARAC
00065Dr 2  A5 49                lda     LINNUM+1
00065Fr 2  85 92                sta     INDEX
000661r 2  C9 19                cmp     #$19
000663r 2  B0 D4                bcs     L28A0
000665r 2               ; <<<<<DANGEROUS CODE>>>>>
000665r 2               ; NOTE THAT IF (A) = $AB ON THE LINE ABOVE,
000665r 2               ; ON.1 WILL COMPARE = AND CAUSE A CATASTROPHIC
000665r 2               ; JUMP TO $22D9 (FOR GOTO), OR OTHER LOCATIONS
000665r 2               ; FOR OTHER CALLS TO LINGET.
000665r 2               ;
000665r 2               ; YOU CAN SEE THIS IS YOU FIRST PUT "BRK" IN $22D9,
000665r 2               ; THEN TYPE "GO TO 437761".
000665r 2               ;
000665r 2               ; ANY VALUE FROM 437760 THROUGH 440319 WILL CAUSE
000665r 2               ; THE PROBLEM.  ($AB00 - $ABFF)
000665r 2               ; <<<<<DANGEROUS CODE>>>>>
000665r 2  A5 48                lda     LINNUM
000667r 2  0A                   asl     a
000668r 2  26 92                rol     INDEX
00066Ar 2  0A                   asl     a
00066Br 2  26 92                rol     INDEX
00066Dr 2  65 48                adc     LINNUM
00066Fr 2  85 48                sta     LINNUM
000671r 2  A5 92                lda     INDEX
000673r 2  65 49                adc     LINNUM+1
000675r 2  85 49                sta     LINNUM+1
000677r 2  06 48                asl     LINNUM
000679r 2  26 49                rol     LINNUM+1
00067Br 2  A5 48                lda     LINNUM
00067Dr 2  65 7A                adc     CHARAC
00067Fr 2  85 48                sta     LINNUM
000681r 2  90 02                bcc     L28EC
000683r 2  E6 49                inc     LINNUM+1
000685r 2               L28EC:
000685r 2  20 E3 00             jsr     CHRGET
000688r 2  4C rr rr             jmp     L28BE

000860r 2               ; to be always taken. For this to happen,
000860r 2               ; the last load must be a 0 for beq
000860r 2               ; and != 0 for bne. The original Microsoft
000860r 2               ; code had ldx/ldy/bne here, which was only
000860r 2               ; correct for a non-ZP INPUTBUFFER. Commodore
000860r 2               ; fixed it in CBMBASIC V1 by swapping the
000860r 2               ; ldx and the ldy. It was broken on KIM,
000860r 2               ; but okay on APPLE and CBM2, because
000860r 2               ; these used a non-ZP INPUTBUFFER.
000860r 2               ; Microsoft fixed this somewhere after KIM
000860r 2               ; and before MICROTAN, by using beq instead
000860r 2               ; of bne in the ZP case.
000860r 2  A2 49                ldx     #<(INPUTBUFFER-1)

000B73r 2  84 7C                sty     EOLPNTR
000B75r 2  20 rr rr             jsr     AYINT
000B78r 2  A5 D4                lda     FAC_LAST-1
000B7Ar 2  45 7C                eor     EOLPNTR
000B7Cr 2  85 7A                sta     CHARAC
000B7Er 2  A5 D5                lda     FAC_LAST
000B80r 2  45 7C                eor     EOLPNTR
000B82r 2  85 7B                sta     ENDCHR
000B84r 2  20 rr rr             jsr     COPY_ARG_TO_FAC
000B87r 2  20 rr rr             jsr     AYINT
000B8Ar 2  A5 D5                lda     FAC_LAST
000B8Cr 2  45 7C                eor     EOLPNTR
000B8Er 2  25 7B                and     ENDCHR
000B90r 2  45 7C                eor     EOLPNTR
000B92r 2  A8                   tay
000B93r 2  A5 D4                lda     FAC_LAST-1
000B95r 2  45 7C                eor     EOLPNTR
000B97r 2  25 7A                and     CHARAC
000B99r 2  45 7C                eor     EOLPNTR
000B9Br 2  4C rr rr             jmp     GIVAYF
000B9Er 2               
000B9Er 2               ; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
000B9Er 2               ; PERFORM RELATIONAL OPERATIONS
000B9Er 2               ; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
000B9Er 2               RELOPS:
000B9Er 2  20 rr rr             jsr     CHKVAL
000BA1r 2  B0 13                bcs     STRCMP
000BA3r 2  A5 DE                lda     ARGSIGN

000BAFr 2  20 rr rr             jsr     FCOMP
000BB2r 2  AA                   tax
000BB3r 2  4C rr rr             jmp     NUMCMP
000BB6r 2               
000BB6r 2               ; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
000BB6r 2               ; STRING COMPARISON

000BBCr 2  20 rr rr             jsr     FREFAC

000BC1r 2  86 D2                stx     FAC+1
000BC3r 2  84 D3                sty     FAC+2
000BC5r 2  A5 DC                lda     ARG_LAST-1
000BC7r 2  A4 DD                ldy     ARG_LAST
000BC9r 2  20 rr rr             jsr     FRETMP
000BCCr 2  86 DC                stx     ARG_LAST-1
000BCEr 2  84 DD                sty     ARG_LAST
000BD0r 2  AA                   tax
000BD1r 2  38                   sec
000BD2r 2  E5 D1                sbc     FAC
000BD4r 2  F0 08                beq     L2E74
000BD6r 2  A9 01                lda     #$01
000BD8r 2  90 04                bcc     L2E74
000BDAr 2  A6 D1                ldx     FAC
000BDCr 2  A9 FF                lda     #$FF
000BDEr 2               L2E74:
000BDEr 2  85 D6                sta     FACSIGN
000BE0r 2  A0 FF                ldy     #$FF
000BE2r 2  E8                   inx

000BE4r 2  CA                   dex
000BE5r 2  D0 07                bne     L2E84
000BE7r 2  A6 D6                ldx     FACSIGN

000BEBr 2  18                   clc
000BECr 2  90 0C                bcc     CMPDONE
000BEEr 2               L2E84:

000BF8r 2  A2 01                ldx     #$01

000BFCr 2  2A                   rol     a
000BFDr 2  25 83                and     CPRMASK
000BFFr 2  F0 02                beq     L2E99
000C01r 2  A9 FF                lda     #$FF
000C03r 2               L2E99:

000C06r 1               .include "var.s"        ; reviewed

000C06r 2               
000C06r 2               ; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
000C06r 2               ; "DIM" STATEMENT
000C06r 2               ; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
000C06r 2               NXDIM:
000C06r 2  20 rr rr             jsr     CHKCOM
000C09r 2               DIM:
000C09r 2  AA                   tax
000C0Ar 2  20 rr rr             jsr     PTRGET2
000C0Dr 2  20 E9 00             jsr     CHRGOT
000C10r 2  D0 F4                bne     NXDIM
000C12r 2  60                   rts
000C13r 2               
000C13r 2               ; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
000C13r 2               ; PTRGET -- GENERAL VARIABLE SCAN
000C13r 2               ;
000C13r 2               ; SCANS VARIABLE NAME AT TXTPTR, AND SEARCHES THE
000C13r 2               ; VARTAB AND ARYTAB FOR THE NAME.
000C13r 2               ; IF NOT FOUND, CREATE VARIABLE OF APPROPRIATE TYPE.
000C13r 2               ; RETURN WITH ADDRESS IN VARPNT AND Y,A
000C13r 2               ;
000C13r 2               ; ACTUAL ACTIVITY CONTROLLED SOMEWHAT BY TWO FLAGS:
000C13r 2               ;	DIMFLG -- NONZERO IF CALLED FROM "DIM"
000C13r 2               ;		ELSE = 0
000C13r 2               ;
000C13r 2               ;	SUBFLG -- = $00
000C13r 2               ;		= $40 IF CALLED FROM "GETARYPT"
000C13r 2               ; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
000C13r 2               PTRGET:
000C13r 2  A2 00                ldx     #$00
000C15r 2  20 E9 00             jsr     CHRGOT
000C18r 2               PTRGET2:
000C18r 2  86 7D                stx     DIMFLG
000C1Ar 2               PTRGET3:
000C1Ar 2  85 B5                sta     VARNAM
000C1Cr 2  20 E9 00             jsr     CHRGOT
000C1Fr 2  20 rr rr             jsr     ISLETC
000C22r 2  B0 03                bcs     NAMOK
000C24r 2               SYNERR3:
000C24r 2  4C rr rr             jmp     SYNERR
000C27r 2               NAMOK:
000C27r 2  A2 00                ldx     #$00
000C29r 2  86 7E                stx     VALTYP
000C2Br 2               .ifndef CONFIG_SMALL
000C2Br 2  86 7F                stx     VALTYP+1
000C2Dr 2               .endif
000C2Dr 2  20 E3 00             jsr     CHRGET
000C30r 2  90 05                bcc     L2ECD
000C32r 2  20 rr rr             jsr     ISLETC
000C35r 2  90 0B                bcc     L2ED8
000C37r 2               L2ECD:
000C37r 2  AA                   tax
000C38r 2               L2ECE:
000C38r 2  20 E3 00             jsr     CHRGET
000C3Br 2  90 FB                bcc     L2ECE
000C3Dr 2  20 rr rr             jsr     ISLETC
000C40r 2  B0 F6                bcs     L2ECE
000C42r 2               L2ED8:
000C42r 2  C9 24                cmp     #$24
000C44r 2               .ifdef CONFIG_SMALL
000C44r 2                       bne     L2EF9
000C44r 2               .else
000C44r 2  D0 06                bne     L2EE2
000C46r 2               .endif
000C46r 2  A9 FF                lda     #$FF
000C48r 2  85 7E                sta     VALTYP
000C4Ar 2               .ifndef CONFIG_SMALL
000C4Ar 2  D0 10                bne     L2EF2
000C4Cr 2               L2EE2:
000C4Cr 2  C9 25                cmp     #$25
000C4Er 2  D0 13                bne     L2EF9
000C50r 2  A5 81                lda     SUBFLG
000C52r 2  D0 D0                bne     SYNERR3
000C54r 2  A9 80                lda     #$80
000C56r 2  85 7F                sta     VALTYP+1
000C58r 2  05 B5                ora     VARNAM
000C5Ar 2  85 B5                sta     VARNAM
000C5Cr 2               L2EF2:
000C5Cr 2               .endif
000C5Cr 2  8A                   txa
000C5Dr 2  09 80                ora     #$80
000C5Fr 2  AA                   tax
000C60r 2  20 E3 00             jsr     CHRGET
000C63r 2               L2EF9:
000C63r 2  86 B6                stx     VARNAM+1
000C65r 2  38                   sec
000C66r 2  05 81                ora     SUBFLG
000C68r 2  E9 28                sbc     #$28
000C6Ar 2  D0 03                bne     L2F05
000C6Cr 2  4C rr rr             jmp     ARRAY
000C6Fr 2               L2F05:
000C6Fr 2  A9 00                lda     #$00
000C71r 2  85 81                sta     SUBFLG
000C73r 2  A5 9D                lda     VARTAB
000C75r 2  A6 9E                ldx     VARTAB+1
000C77r 2  A0 00                ldy     #$00
000C79r 2               L2F0F:
000C79r 2  86 D0                stx     LOWTR+1
000C7Br 2               L2F11:
000C7Br 2  85 CF                sta     LOWTR
000C7Dr 2  E4 A0                cpx     ARYTAB+1
000C7Fr 2  D0 04                bne     L2F1B
000C81r 2  C5 9F                cmp     ARYTAB
000C83r 2  F0 22                beq     NAMENOTFOUND
000C85r 2               L2F1B:
000C85r 2  A5 B5                lda     VARNAM
000C87r 2  D1 CF                cmp     (LOWTR),y
000C89r 2  D0 08                bne     L2F29
000C8Br 2  A5 B6                lda     VARNAM+1
000C8Dr 2  C8                   iny
000C8Er 2  D1 CF                cmp     (LOWTR),y
000C90r 2  F0 62                beq     SET_VARPNT_AND_YA
000C92r 2  88                   dey
000C93r 2               L2F29:
000C93r 2  18                   clc
000C94r 2  A5 CF                lda     LOWTR
000C96r 2  69 07                adc     #BYTES_PER_VARIABLE
000C98r 2  90 E1                bcc     L2F11
000C9Ar 2  E8                   inx
000C9Br 2  D0 DC                bne     L2F0F
000C9Dr 2               
000C9Dr 2               ; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
000C9Dr 2               ; CHECK IF (A) IS ASCII LETTER A-Z
000C9Dr 2               ;
000C9Dr 2               ; RETURN CARRY = 1 IF A-Z
000C9Dr 2               ;	= 0 IF NOT
000C9Dr 2               ; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
000C9Dr 2               ISLETC:
000C9Dr 2  C9 41                cmp     #$41
000C9Fr 2  90 05                bcc     L2F3C
000CA1r 2  E9 5B                sbc     #$5B
000CA3r 2  38                   sec
000CA4r 2  E9 A5                sbc     #$A5
000CA6r 2               L2F3C:
000CA6r 2  60                   rts
000CA7r 2               
000CA7r 2               ; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
000CA7r 2               ; VARIABLE NOT FOUND, SO MAKE ONE
000CA7r 2               ; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
000CA7r 2               NAMENOTFOUND:
000CA7r 2  68                   pla
000CA8r 2  48                   pha
000CA9r 2  C9 rr                cmp     #<FRM_VARIABLE_CALL
000CABr 2  D0 05                bne     MAKENEWVARIABLE
000CADr 2               .ifdef CONFIG_SAFE_NAMENOTFOUND
000CADr 2                       tsx
000CADr 2                       lda     STACK+2,x
000CADr 2                       cmp     #>FRM_VARIABLE_CALL
000CADr 2                       bne     MAKENEWVARIABLE
000CADr 2               .endif
000CADr 2               LD015:
000CADr 2  A9 rr                lda     #<C_ZERO
000CAFr 2  A0 rr                ldy     #>C_ZERO
000CB1r 2  60                   rts
000CB2r 2               
000CB2r 2               ; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
000CB2r 2               .ifndef CONFIG_2
000CB2r 2               C_ZERO:
000CB2r 2                       .byte   $00,$00
000CB2r 2               .endif
000CB2r 2               
000CB2r 2               ; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
000CB2r 2               ; MAKE A NEW SIMPLE VARIABLE
000CB2r 2               ;
000CB2r 2               ; MOVE ARRAYS UP 7 BYTES TO MAKE ROOM FOR NEW VARIABLE
000CB2r 2               ; ENTER 7-BYTE VARIABLE DATA IN THE HOLE
000CB2r 2               ; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
000CB2r 2               MAKENEWVARIABLE:
000CB2r 2  A5 9F                lda     ARYTAB
000CB4r 2  A4 A0                ldy     ARYTAB+1
000CB6r 2  85 CF                sta     LOWTR
000CB8r 2  84 D0                sty     LOWTR+1
000CBAr 2  A5 A1                lda     STREND
000CBCr 2  A4 A2                ldy     STREND+1
000CBEr 2  85 CA                sta     HIGHTR
000CC0r 2  84 CB                sty     HIGHTR+1
000CC2r 2  18                   clc
000CC3r 2  69 07                adc     #BYTES_PER_VARIABLE
000CC5r 2  90 01                bcc     L2F68
000CC7r 2  C8                   iny
000CC8r 2               L2F68:
000CC8r 2  85 C8                sta     HIGHDS
000CCAr 2  84 C9                sty     HIGHDS+1
000CCCr 2  20 rr rr             jsr     BLTU
000CCFr 2  A5 C8                lda     HIGHDS
000CD1r 2  A4 C9                ldy     HIGHDS+1
000CD3r 2  C8                   iny
000CD4r 2  85 9F                sta     ARYTAB
000CD6r 2  84 A0                sty     ARYTAB+1
000CD8r 2  A0 00                ldy     #$00
000CDAr 2  A5 B5                lda     VARNAM
000CDCr 2  91 CF                sta     (LOWTR),y
000CDEr 2  C8                   iny
000CDFr 2  A5 B6                lda     VARNAM+1
000CE1r 2  91 CF                sta     (LOWTR),y
000CE3r 2  A9 00                lda     #$00
000CE5r 2  C8                   iny
000CE6r 2  91 CF                sta     (LOWTR),y
000CE8r 2  C8                   iny
000CE9r 2  91 CF                sta     (LOWTR),y
000CEBr 2  C8                   iny
000CECr 2  91 CF                sta     (LOWTR),y
000CEEr 2  C8                   iny
000CEFr 2  91 CF                sta     (LOWTR),y
000CF1r 2               .ifndef CONFIG_SMALL
000CF1r 2  C8                   iny
000CF2r 2  91 CF                sta     (LOWTR),y
000CF4r 2               .endif
000CF4r 2               
000CF4r 2               ; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
000CF4r 2               ; PUT ADDRESS OF VALUE OF VARIABLE IN VARPNT AND Y,A
000CF4r 2               ; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
000CF4r 2               SET_VARPNT_AND_YA:
000CF4r 2  A5 CF                lda     LOWTR
000CF6r 2  18                   clc
000CF7r 2  69 02                adc     #$02
000CF9r 2  A4 D0                ldy     LOWTR+1
000CFBr 2  90 01                bcc     L2F9E
000CFDr 2  C8                   iny
000CFEr 2               L2F9E:
000CFEr 2  85 B7                sta     VARPNT
000D00r 2  84 B8                sty     VARPNT+1
000D02r 2  60                   rts
000D03r 2               
000D03r 1               .include "array.s"      ; reviewed
000D03r 2               ;
000D03r 2               ;  PGS 2/13/26 - removed most conditionals for old machine versions
000D03r 2               ;
000D03r 2               ;
000D03r 2               ;  array.s
000D03r 2               ;
000D03r 2               .segment "CODE"
000D03r 2               
000D03r 2               ; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
000D03r 2               ; COMPUTE ADDRESS OF FIRST VALUE IN ARRAY
000D03r 2               ; ARYPNT = (LOWTR) + #DIMS*2 + 5
000D03r 2               ; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
000D03r 2               GETARY:
000D03r 2  A5 7C                lda     EOLPNTR
000D05r 2  0A                   asl     a
000D06r 2  69 05                adc     #$05
000D08r 2  65 CF                adc     LOWTR
000D0Ar 2  A4 D0                ldy     LOWTR+1
000D0Cr 2  90 01                bcc     L2FAF
000D0Er 2  C8                   iny
000D0Fr 2               L2FAF:

000D11r 2  84 C9                sty     HIGHDS+1
000D13r 2  60                   rts
000D14r 2               
000D14r 2               ; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
000D14r 2               NEG32768:
000D14r 2  90 80 00 00          .byte   $90,$80,$00,$00,$00   ; incorporating v2C bugfix
000D18r 2  00           
000D19r 2               
000D19r 2               ; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
000D19r 2               ; EVALUATE NUMERIC FORMULA AT TXTPTR
000D19r 2               ; CONVERTING RESULT TO INTEGER 0 <= X <= 32767
000D19r 2               ; IN FAC+3,4
000D19r 2               ; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
000D19r 2               MAKINT:
000D19r 2  20 E3 00             jsr     CHRGET
000D1Cr 2               .ifdef CONFIG_2
000D1Cr 2  20 rr rr             jsr     FRMEVL
000D1Fr 2               .else
000D1Fr 2                       jsr     FRMNUM
000D1Fr 2               .endif
000D1Fr 2               
000D1Fr 2               ; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
000D1Fr 2               ; CONVERT FAC TO INTEGER
000D1Fr 2               ; MUST BE POSITIVE AND LESS THAN 32768
000D1Fr 2               ; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
000D1Fr 2               MKINT:
000D1Fr 2               .ifdef CONFIG_2
000D1Fr 2  20 rr rr             jsr     CHKNUM
000D22r 2               .endif
000D22r 2  A5 D6                lda     FACSIGN
000D24r 2  30 0D                bmi     MI1
000D26r 2               
000D26r 2               ; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
000D26r 2               ; CONVERT FAC TO INTEGER
000D26r 2               ; MUST BE -32767 <= FAC <= 32767
000D26r 2               ; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
000D26r 2               AYINT:
000D26r 2  A5 D1                lda     FAC
000D28r 2  C9 90                cmp     #$90
000D2Ar 2  90 09                bcc     MI2
000D2Cr 2  A9 rr                lda     #<NEG32768
000D2Er 2  A0 rr                ldy     #>NEG32768
000D30r 2  20 rr rr             jsr     FCOMP
000D33r 2               MI1:
000D33r 2  D0 7A                bne     IQERR
000D35r 2               MI2:
000D35r 2  4C rr rr             jmp     QINT
000D38r 2               
000D38r 2               ; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
000D38r 2               ; LOCATE ARRAY ELEMENT OR CREATE AN ARRAY
000D38r 2               ; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
000D38r 2               ARRAY:
000D38r 2  A5 7D                lda     DIMFLG
000D3Ar 2               .ifndef CONFIG_SMALL
000D3Ar 2  05 7F                ora     VALTYP+1
000D3Cr 2               .endif
000D3Cr 2  48                   pha
000D3Dr 2  A5 7E                lda     VALTYP
000D3Fr 2  48                   pha
000D40r 2  A0 00                ldy     #$00
000D42r 2               L2FDE:
000D42r 2  98                   tya
000D43r 2  48                   pha
000D44r 2  A5 B6                lda     VARNAM+1
000D46r 2  48                   pha
000D47r 2  A5 B5                lda     VARNAM
000D49r 2  48                   pha
000D4Ar 2  20 rr rr             jsr     MAKINT
000D4Dr 2  68                   pla
000D4Er 2  85 B5                sta     VARNAM
000D50r 2  68                   pla
000D51r 2  85 B6                sta     VARNAM+1
000D53r 2  68                   pla
000D54r 2  A8                   tay
000D55r 2  BA                   tsx
000D56r 2  BD 02 01             lda     STACK+2,x
000D59r 2  48                   pha
000D5Ar 2  BD 01 01             lda     STACK+1,x
000D5Dr 2  48                   pha
000D5Er 2  A5 D4                lda     FAC_LAST-1
000D60r 2  9D 02 01             sta     STACK+2,x
000D63r 2  A5 D5                lda     FAC_LAST
000D65r 2  9D 01 01             sta     STACK+1,x
000D68r 2  C8                   iny
000D69r 2  20 E9 00             jsr     CHRGOT
000D6Cr 2  C9 2C                cmp     #$2C
000D6Er 2  F0 D2                beq     L2FDE
000D70r 2  84 7C                sty     EOLPNTR
000D72r 2  20 rr rr             jsr     CHKCLS
000D75r 2  68                   pla
000D76r 2  85 7E                sta     VALTYP
000D78r 2  68                   pla
000D79r 2               .ifndef CONFIG_SMALL
000D79r 2  85 7F                sta     VALTYP+1
000D7Br 2  29 7F                and     #$7F
000D7Dr 2               .endif
000D7Dr 2  85 7D                sta     DIMFLG
000D7Fr 2               ; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
000D7Fr 2               ; SEARCH ARRAY TABLE FOR THIS ARRAY NAME
000D7Fr 2               ; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
000D7Fr 2  A6 9F                ldx     ARYTAB
000D81r 2  A5 A0                lda     ARYTAB+1
000D83r 2               L301F:
000D83r 2  86 CF                stx     LOWTR
000D85r 2  85 D0                sta     LOWTR+1
000D87r 2  C5 A2                cmp     STREND+1
000D89r 2  D0 04                bne     L302B
000D8Br 2  E4 A1                cpx     STREND
000D8Dr 2  F0 39                beq     MAKE_NEW_ARRAY
000D8Fr 2               L302B:
000D8Fr 2  A0 00                ldy     #$00
000D91r 2  B1 CF                lda     (LOWTR),y
000D93r 2  C8                   iny
000D94r 2  C5 B5                cmp     VARNAM
000D96r 2  D0 06                bne     L303A
000D98r 2  A5 B6                lda     VARNAM+1
000D9Ar 2  D1 CF                cmp     (LOWTR),y
000D9Cr 2  F0 16                beq     USE_OLD_ARRAY
000D9Er 2               L303A:
000D9Er 2  C8                   iny
000D9Fr 2  B1 CF                lda     (LOWTR),y
000DA1r 2  18                   clc
000DA2r 2  65 CF                adc     LOWTR
000DA4r 2  AA                   tax
000DA5r 2  C8                   iny
000DA6r 2  B1 CF                lda     (LOWTR),y
000DA8r 2  65 D0                adc     LOWTR+1
000DAAr 2  90 D7                bcc     L301F
000DACr 2               
000DACr 2               ; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
000DACr 2               ; ERROR:  BAD SUBSCRIPTS
000DACr 2               ; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
000DACr 2               SUBERR:
000DACr 2  A2 3B                ldx     #ERR_BADSUBS
000DAEr 2  2C                   .byte   $2C
000DAFr 2               
000DAFr 2               ; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
000DAFr 2               ; ERROR:  ILLEGAL QUANTITY
000DAFr 2               ; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
000DAFr 2               IQERR:
000DAFr 2  A2 20                ldx     #ERR_ILLQTY
000DB1r 2               JER:
000DB1r 2  4C rr rr             jmp     ERROR
000DB4r 2               
000DB4r 2               ; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
000DB4r 2               ; FOUND THE ARRAY
000DB4r 2               ; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
000DB4r 2               USE_OLD_ARRAY:
000DB4r 2  A2 42                ldx     #ERR_REDIMD
000DB6r 2  A5 7D                lda     DIMFLG
000DB8r 2  D0 F7                bne     JER
000DBAr 2  20 rr rr             jsr     GETARY
000DBDr 2  A5 7C                lda     EOLPNTR
000DBFr 2  A0 04                ldy     #$04
000DC1r 2  D1 CF                cmp     (LOWTR),y
000DC3r 2  D0 E7                bne     SUBERR
000DC5r 2  4C rr rr             jmp     FIND_ARRAY_ELEMENT
000DC8r 2               
000DC8r 2               ; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
000DC8r 2               ; CREATE A NEW ARRAY, UNLESS CALLED FROM GETARYPT
000DC8r 2               ; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
000DC8r 2               MAKE_NEW_ARRAY:
000DC8r 2  20 rr rr             jsr     GETARY
000DCBr 2  20 rr rr             jsr     REASON
000DCEr 2  A9 00                lda     #$00
000DD0r 2  A8                   tay
000DD1r 2  85 E2                sta     STRNG2+1
000DD3r 2  A2 05                ldx     #BYTES_PER_ELEMENT
000DD5r 2               .if .def(CONFIG_SMALL) && (!.def(CONFIG_2))
000DD5r 2                       stx     STRNG2
000DD5r 2               .endif
000DD5r 2  A5 B5                lda     VARNAM
000DD7r 2  91 CF                sta     (LOWTR),y
000DD9r 2               .ifndef CONFIG_SMALL

0017ACr 2               ; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
0017ACr 2               ; FLOAT UNSIGNED VALUE IN FAC+1,2
0017ACr 2               ; (X) = EXPONENT
0017ACr 2               ; C=0 TO MAKE VALUE NEGATIVE
0017ACr 2               ; C=1 TO MAKE VALUE POSITIVE

0017FEr 2               
0017FEr 2               ; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
0017FEr 2               ; QUICK INTEGER FUNCTION
0017FEr 2               ;
0017FEr 2               ; CONVERTS FP VALUE IN FAC TO INTEGER VALUE
0017FEr 2               ; IN FAC+1...FAC+4, BY SHIFTING RIGHT WITH SIGN
0017FEr 2               ; EXTENSION UNTIL FRACTIONAL BITS ARE OUT.
0017FEr 2               ;
0017FEr 2               ; THIS SUBROUTINE ASSUMES THE EXPONENT < 32.
0017FEr 2               ; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
0017FEr 2               QINT:
0017FEr 2  A5 D1                lda     FAC
001800r 2  F0 4A                beq     QINT3
001802r 2  38                   sec
001803r 2  E9 A0                sbc     #120+8*BYTES_FP
001805r 2  24 D6                bit     FACSIGN
001807r 2  10 09                bpl     L3B27
001809r 2  AA                   tax

00180Cr 2  85 D8                sta     SHIFTSIGNEXT
00180Er 2  20 rr rr             jsr     COMPLEMENT_FAC_MANTISSA
001811r 2  8A                   txa

001814r 2  C9 F9                cmp     #$F9
001816r 2  10 06                bpl     QINT2
001818r 2  20 rr rr             jsr     SHIFT_RIGHT
00181Br 2  84 D8                sty     SHIFTSIGNEXT

001829r 2  20 rr rr             jsr     SHIFT_RIGHT4
00182Cr 2  84 D8                sty     SHIFTSIGNEXT
00182Er 2  60                   rts

00182Fr 2               ; "INT" FUNCTION
00182Fr 2               ;

001B68r 2               ; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
001B68r 2               ; ODD POLYNOMIAL SUBROUTINE
001B68r 2               ;
001B68r 2               ; F(X) = X * P(X^2)
001B68r 2               ;
001B68r 2               ; WHERE:  X IS VALUE IN FAC
001B68r 2               ;	Y,A POINTS AT COEFFICIENT TABLE
001B68r 2               ;	FIRST BYTE OF COEFF. TABLE IS N
001B68r 2               ;	COEFFICIENTS FOLLOW, HIGHEST POWER FIRST
001B68r 2               ;
001B68r 2               ; P(X^2) COMPUTED USING NORMAL POLYNOMIAL SUBROUTINE
001B68r 2               ; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
001B68r 2               POLYNOMIAL_ODD:
001B68r 2  85 E1                sta     STRNG2
001B6Ar 2  84 E2                sty     STRNG2+1
001B6Cr 2  20 rr rr             jsr     STORE_FAC_IN_TEMP1_ROUNDED
001B6Fr 2  A9 C7                lda     #TEMP1X
001B71r 2  20 rr rr             jsr     FMULT
001B74r 2  20 rr rr             jsr     SERMAIN
001B77r 2  A9 C7                lda     #TEMP1X
001B79r 2  A0 00                ldy     #$00
001B7Br 2  4C rr rr             jmp     FMULT
001B7Er 2               
001B7Er 2               ; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
001B7Er 2               ; NORMAL POLYNOMIAL SUBROUTINE
001B7Er 2               ;
001B7Er 2               ; P(X) = C(0)*X^N + C(1)*X^(N-1) + ... + C(N)
001B7Er 2               ;
001B7Er 2               ; WHERE:  X IS VALUE IN FAC
001B7Er 2               ;	Y,A POINTS AT COEFFICIENT TABLE
001B7Er 2               ;	FIRST BYTE OF COEFF. TABLE IS N
001B7Er 2               ;	COEFFICIENTS FOLLOW, HIGHEST POWER FIRST
001B7Er 2               ; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
001B7Er 2               POLYNOMIAL:
001B7Er 2  85 E1                sta     STRNG2
001B80r 2  84 E2                sty     STRNG2+1
001B82r 2               SERMAIN:
001B82r 2  20 rr rr             jsr     STORE_FAC_IN_TEMP2_ROUNDED
001B85r 2  B1 E1                lda     (STRNG2),y
001B87r 2  85 D7                sta     SERLEN
001B89r 2  A4 E1                ldy     STRNG2
001B8Br 2  C8                   iny
001B8Cr 2  98                   tya
001B8Dr 2  D0 02                bne     L3EBA
001B8Fr 2  E6 E2                inc     STRNG2+1
001B91r 2               L3EBA:
001B91r 2  85 E1                sta     STRNG2
001B93r 2  A4 E2                ldy     STRNG2+1
001B95r 2               L3EBE:
001B95r 2  20 rr rr             jsr     FMULT
001B98r 2  A5 E1                lda     STRNG2
001B9Ar 2  A4 E2                ldy     STRNG2+1

001B9Dr 2  69 05                adc     #BYTES_FP
001B9Fr 2  90 01                bcc     L3ECB

001BFDr 2               ; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
001BFDr 2               SIN:
001BFDr 2  20 rr rr             jsr     COPY_FAC_TO_ARG_ROUNDED
001C00r 2  A9 rr                lda     #<CON_PI_DOUB
001C02r 2  A0 rr                ldy     #>CON_PI_DOUB

001C11r 2  85 DF                sta     STRNG1
001C13r 2  20 rr rr             jsr     FSUBT
001C16r 2               ; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
001C16r 2               ; (FAC) = ANGLE AS A FRACTION OF A FULL CIRCLE
001C16r 2               ;
001C16r 2               ; NOW FOLD THE RANGE INTO A QUARTER CIRCLE
001C16r 2               ;
001C16r 2               ; <<< THERE ARE MUCH SIMPLER WAYS TO DO THIS >>>
001C16r 2               ; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
001C16r 2  A9 rr                lda     #<QUARTER
001C18r 2  A0 rr                ldy     #>QUARTER
001C1Ar 2  20 rr rr             jsr     FSUB
001C1Dr 2  A5 D6                lda     FACSIGN
001C1Fr 2  48                   pha
001C20r 2  10 0D                bpl     SIN1
001C22r 2  20 rr rr             jsr     FADDH
001C25r 2  A5 D6                lda     FACSIGN
001C27r 2  30 09                bmi     L3F5B
001C29r 2  A5 83                lda     CPRMASK
001C2Br 2  49 FF                eor     #$FF
001C2Dr 2  85 83                sta     CPRMASK
001C2Fr 2               ; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
001C2Fr 2               ; IF FALL THRU, RANGE IS 0...1/2
001C2Fr 2               ; IF BRANCH HERE, RANGE IS 0...1/4
001C2Fr 2               ; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
001C2Fr 2               SIN1:
001C2Fr 2  20 rr rr             jsr     NEGOP
001C32r 2               ; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
001C32r 2               ; IF FALL THRU, RANGE IS -1/2...0
001C32r 2               ; IF BRANCH HERE, RANGE IS -1/4...0
001C32r 2               ; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
001C32r 2               L3F5B:
001C32r 2  A9 rr                lda     #<QUARTER
001C34r 2  A0 rr                ldy     #>QUARTER
001C36r 2  20 rr rr             jsr     FADD
001C39r 2  68                   pla

000062r 2               ;  Ben Eater ran
000062r 2               ;       jsr     LCDINIT
000062r 2               ;
000062r 2               ;  ...here to init the LCD
000062r 2               ;
000062r 2               
000062r 2               ; All non-CONFIG_SMALL versions of BASIC have
000062r 2               ; the same bug here: While the number of bytes
000062r 2               ; to be copied is correct for CONFIG_SMALL,
000062r 2               ; it is one byte short on non-CONFIG_SMALL:
000062r 2               ; It seems the "ldx" value below has been
000062r 2               ; hardcoded. So on these configurations,
000062r 2               ; the last byte of GENERIC_RNDSEED, which
000062r 2               ; is 5 bytes instead of 4, does not get copied -
000062r 2               ; which is nothing major, because it is just
000062r 2               ; the least significant 8 bits of the mantissa
000062r 2               ; of the random number seed.
000062r 2               ; KBD added three bytes to CHRGET and removed
000062r 2               ; the random number seed, but only adjusted
000062r 2               ; the number of bytes by adding 3 - this
000062r 2               ; copies four bytes too many, which is no
000062r 2               ; problem.
000062r 2               .ifdef CONFIG_SMALL
000062r 2                       ldx     #GENERIC_CHRGET_END-GENERIC_CHRGET
000062r 2               .else
000062r 2  A2 1C                ldx     #GENERIC_CHRGET_END-GENERIC_CHRGET-1 ; XXX
000064r 2               .endif

000172r 2               .macro debug_varstr varble
000172r 2               .ident(.concat(.string(varble), "TXT")):
000172r 2                       .byte $0D, $0A
000172r 2                       .byte .concat(.string(varble), " : $")
000172r 2                       .byte 0
000172r 2               .endmacro

000172r 2               .macro OUTDEBUGLN varble, len
000172r 2                   .local Loop
000172r 2                       lda #<.ident(.concat(.string(varble), "TXT"))
000172r 2                       ldy #>.ident(.concat(.string(varble), "TXT"))
000172r 2                       jsr STROUT
000172r 2                       ldy #0
000172r 2                       ldx #len
000172r 2                  .if len > 0
000172r 2               Loop:
000172r 2                       iny
000172r 2                       lda varble, y
000172r 2                       jsr WRITE_BYTE
000172r 2                       dex
000172r 2                       bne Loop
000172r 2                  .endif
000172r 2               .endmacro

0003CDr 3               
0003CDr 3               ZP_WM_DASTATE = $30
0003CDr 3               ZP_WM_MODE    = $2F
0003CDr 3               ZP_WM_HVP     = $2D
0003CDr 3               ZP_WM_ST      = $2B
0003CDr 3               ZP_XAM        = $24                   ; Last "opened" location
0003CDr 3               ZP_Y_SAVE     = $22
0003CDr 3               ;
0003CDr 3               .segment "DEBUG"
0003CDr 3               
0003CDr 3               DISASM:                                          ; FOR NOW
0003CDr 3  60                          rts
0003CEr 3               MON_START:
0003CEr 3               WOZGO:
0003CEr 3  D8                           cld                     ; Clear decimal arithmetic mode.
0003CFr 3  08                           php
0003D0r 3  48                           pha
0003D1r 3  DA                           phx
0003D2r 3  5A                           phy
0003D3r 3  58                           cli                     ; Enable interrupts
0003D4r 3  64 30                        stz     ZP_WM_DASTATE
0003D6r 3  80 10                        bra     is_start
0003D8r 3               
0003D8r 3               not_cr:
0003D8r 3  C9 08                        cmp     #BACKSPC
0003DAr 3  F0 27                        beq     is_backspace
0003DCr 3  C9 1B                        cmp     #$1B
0003DEr 3  F0 03                        beq     IS_ESCAPE
0003E0r 3  C8                           iny                     ; Advance text index.
0003E1r 3  10 23                        bpl     get_next_char  ; Auto ESC if line longer than 127.
0003E3r 3               IS_ESCAPE:
0003E3r 3  A9 5C                        lda     #$5C
0003E5r 3  20 03 F8                     jsr     MONCOUT
0003E8r 3               is_start:
0003E8r 3  A9 0D                        lda     #$0D
0003EAr 3  20 03 F8                     jsr     MONCOUT
0003EDr 3  A9 0A                        lda     #$0A
0003EFr 3  20 03 F8                     jsr     MONCOUT
0003F2r 3               
0003F2r 3               get_line:
0003F2r 3  A9 0D                        lda     #$0D
0003F4r 3  20 03 F8                     jsr     MONCOUT
0003F7r 3  A9 0A                        lda     #$0A
0003F9r 3  20 03 F8                     jsr     MONCOUT
0003FCr 3  A9 5D                        lda     #$5D
0003FEr 3  20 03 F8                     jsr     MONCOUT
000401r 3  A0 01                        ldy     #1              ; Initialize text index.
000403r 3               
000403r 3               is_backspace:
000403r 3  88                           dey             ; Back up text index.
000404r 3  30 EC                        bmi     get_line       ; Beyond start of line, reinitialize.
000406r 3               
000406r 3               get_next_char:
000406r 3  20 00 F8                     jsr     MONRDKEY
000409r 3  90 FB                        bcc     get_next_char
00040Br 3  99 rr rr                     sta     INPUT_BUFFER,y            ; Add to text buffer.
00040Er 3  C9 0D                        cmp     #$0D
000410r 3  D0 C6                        bne     not_cr
000412r 3  A0 FF                        ldy     #$FF            ; Reset text index.  Will iny shortly...
000414r 3  A9 00                        lda     #$00            ; For ZP_XAM mode.
000416r 3  AA                           tax                     ; .X=0.
000417r 3               set_block:
000417r 3  0A                           asl
000418r 3               set_store:
000418r 3  0A                           asl                     ; Leaves $7B if setting STOR mode.
000419r 3               set_mode:
000419r 3  85 2F                        sta     ZP_WM_MODE      ; $00 = ZP_XAM, $74 = STOR, $B8 = BLOK ZP_XAM.
00041Br 3               skip_delim:
00041Br 3  C8                           iny                     ; Advance text index.
00041Cr 3               NEXT_ITEM:
00041Cr 3  B9 rr rr                     lda     INPUT_BUFFER,y            ; Get character.
00041Fr 3  C9 0D                        cmp     #$0D            ; CR?
000421r 3  F0 CF                        beq     get_line       ; Yes, done with this line.
000423r 3  C9 2E                        cmp     #'.'
000425r 3  90 F4                        bcc     skip_delim     ; Skip delimiter.
000427r 3  F0 EE                        beq     set_block      ; Set BLOCK ZP_XAM mode.
000429r 3  C9 3A                        cmp     #':'
00042Br 3  F0 EB                        beq     set_store      ; Yes, set STOR mode.
00042Dr 3  C9 4C                        cmp     #'L'
00042Fr 3  F0 64                        beq     DO_DISASM    ; Disassemble 1 or more instructions at current address/range
000431r 3  C9 5E                        cmp     #'^'
000433r 3  D0 03                        bne     WZCONT
000435r 3  4C rr rr                     jmp     WOZEXIT          ; RTS
000438r 3  C9 52        WZCONT:         cmp     #'R'
00043Ar 3  F0 50                        beq     run_prog       ; Yes, run user program
00043Cr 3  C9 54                        cmp     #'T'            ; T, U, V, or W registers?
00043Er 3  90 0F                        bcc     not_tuvw       ;
000440r 3  C9 58                        cmp     #'X'            ;
000442r 3  B0 0B                        bcs     not_tuvw       ;
000444r 3  69 9C                        adc     #($F0-'T')      ; T=FFF0, U=FFF1, V=FFF2, W=FFF3
000446r 3  85 2D                        sta     ZP_WM_HVP       ;
000448r 3  A9 FF                        lda     #$FF            ;
00044Ar 3  85 2E                        sta     ZP_WM_HVP + 1   ;
00044Cr 3  C8                           iny                     ; skip the mnemonic
00044Dr 3  80 2D                        bra     not_hex_or_escape
00044Fr 3               
00044Fr 3               not_tuvw:
00044Fr 3  84 22                        sty     ZP_Y_SAVE       ; Save Y for comparison
000451r 3  86 2D                        stx     ZP_WM_HVP       ; $00 -> Low byte of HPV
000453r 3  86 2E                        stx     ZP_WM_HVP + 1   ; ...and High byte.
000455r 3               
000455r 3               next_hex:
000455r 3  B9 rr rr                     lda     INPUT_BUFFER,y     ; Get character for hex test.
000458r 3  49 30                        eor     #'0'               ; Map digits to $0-9.
00045Ar 3  C9 0A                        cmp     #10                ; Digit?
00045Cr 3  90 06                        bcc     is_digit           ; Yes.
00045Er 3  69 88                        adc     #$88               ; Map letter "A"-"F" to $FA-FF.
000460r 3  C9 FA                        cmp     #$FA               ; Hex letter?
000462r 3  90 11                        bcc     not_hex            ; No, character not hex.
000464r 3               
000464r 3               is_digit:
000464r 3  0A                           asl                     ; LSD to MSD of A.
000465r 3  0A                           asl
000466r 3  0A                           asl
000467r 3  0A                           asl
000468r 3  A2 04                        ldx     #4              ; Shift count.
00046Ar 3               
00046Ar 3               hex_shift:
00046Ar 3  0A                           asl                     ; Hex digit left, MSB to carry.
00046Br 3  26 2D                        rol     ZP_WM_HVP       ; Rotate into LSD.
00046Dr 3  26 2E                        rol     ZP_WM_HVP + 1   ; Rotate into MSD's.
00046Fr 3  CA                           dex                     ; Done 4 shifts?
000470r 3  D0 F8                        bne     hex_shift      ; No, loop.
000472r 3  C8                           iny                     ; Advance text index.
000473r 3  D0 E0                        bne     next_hex       ; Always taken. Check next character for hex.
000475r 3               
000475r 3               not_hex:
000475r 3  C4 22                        cpy     ZP_Y_SAVE       ; Check if HPV empty (no hex digits).
000477r 3  D0 03                        bne     not_hex_or_escape      ; Yes, generate ESC sequence.
000479r 3  4C rr rr                     jmp     IS_ESCAPE
00047Cr 3               not_hex_or_escape:
00047Cr 3  24 2F                        bit     ZP_WM_MODE      ; Test ZP_WM_MODE byte.
00047Er 3  50 19                        bvc     not_store      ; B6=0 is STOR, 1 is ZP_XAM and BLOCK ZP_XAM.
000480r 3  A5 2D                        lda     ZP_WM_HVP       ; LSD's of hex data.
000482r 3  92 2B                        sta     (ZP_WM_ST)      ; Store to current 'store index'.
000484r 3  E6 2B                        inc     ZP_WM_ST        ; Increment store index.
000486r 3  D0 94                        bne     NEXT_ITEM; Get next item (no carry).
000488r 3  E6 2C                        inc     ZP_WM_ST + 1    ; Add carry to 'store index' high order.
00048Ar 3               
00048Ar 3               to_next_item:
00048Ar 3  80 90                        bra     NEXT_ITEM      ; Get next command item.
00048Cr 3               run_prog:
00048Cr 3  A9 00                        lda     #>ZP_XAM
00048Er 3  48                           pha
00048Fr 3  A9 24                        lda     #<ZP_XAM
000491r 3  48                           pha
000492r 3  6C rr rr                     jmp     (MON_START)
000495r 3               DO_DISASM:
000495r 3  A9 01                        lda     #1
000497r 3  85 30                        sta     ZP_WM_DASTATE
000499r 3               not_store:
000499r 3  30 39                        bmi     examine_next   ; B7 = 0 for ZP_XAM, 1 for BLOCK ZP_XAM.
00049Br 3  A2 02                        ldx     #2              ; Byte count.
00049Dr 3               
00049Dr 3               set_addr:
00049Dr 3  B5 2C                        lda     ZP_WM_HVP - 1,x ; Copy hex data to
00049Fr 3  95 2A                        sta     ZP_WM_ST - 1,x  ;   'store index'.
0004A1r 3  95 23                        sta     ZP_XAM - 1,x    ; And to 'ZP_XAM index'.
0004A3r 3  CA                           dex                     ; Next of 2 bytes.
0004A4r 3  D0 F7                        bne     set_addr       ; Loop unless X = 0.
0004A6r 3               
0004A6r 3               print_next:
0004A6r 3  D0 19                        bne     print_data     ; NE means no address to print.
0004A8r 3  A9 0D                        lda     #$0D
0004AAr 3  20 03 F8                     jsr     MONCOUT
0004ADr 3  A9 0A                        lda     #$0A
0004AFr 3  20 03 F8                     jsr     MONCOUT
0004B2r 3  A5 25                        lda     ZP_XAM + 1      ; Print 'examine index' high-order byte.
0004B4r 3  20 06 F8                     jsr     WRITE_BYTE
0004B7r 3  A5 24                        lda     ZP_XAM          ; Print 'examine index' low-order byte.
0004B9r 3  20 06 F8                     jsr     WRITE_BYTE
0004BCr 3  A9 3A                        lda     #':'    ; Print a ':'.
0004BEr 3  20 03 F8                     jsr     MONCOUT
0004C1r 3               
0004C1r 3               print_data:
0004C1r 3  A5 30                        lda     ZP_WM_DASTATE
0004C3r 3  F0 05                        beq     print_bytes
0004C5r 3  20 rr rr                     jsr     DISASM
0004C8r 3  80 0A                        bra     examine_next
0004CAr 3               
0004CAr 3               print_bytes:
0004CAr 3  A9 20                        lda     #$20
0004CCr 3  20 03 F8                     jsr     MONCOUT                  ; Print a ' '.
0004CFr 3  A1 24                        lda     (ZP_XAM,x)
0004D1r 3  20 06 F8                     jsr     WRITE_BYTE         ; Print the byte at 'examine index'.
0004D4r 3               
0004D4r 3               examine_next:
0004D4r 3  86 2F                        stx     ZP_WM_MODE      ; 0 -> ZP_WM_MODE (ZP_XAM mode).
0004D6r 3  A5 24                        lda     ZP_XAM
0004D8r 3  C5 2D                        cmp     ZP_WM_HVP       ; Compare 'examine index' to hex data.
0004DAr 3  A5 25                        lda     ZP_XAM + 1
0004DCr 3  E5 2E                        sbc     ZP_WM_HVP + 1
0004DEr 3  B0 AA                        bcs     to_next_item   ; Not less, so no more data to output.
0004E0r 3  E6 24                        inc     ZP_XAM
0004E2r 3  D0 02                        bne     mod_8_check    ; Increment 'examine index'.
0004E4r 3  E6 25                        inc     ZP_XAM + 1
0004E6r 3               
0004E6r 3               mod_8_check:
0004E6r 3  A5 24                        lda     ZP_XAM          ; Check low-order 'examine index' byte
0004E8r 3  29 07                        and     #7              ; For MOD 8 = 0
0004EAr 3  10 BA                        bpl     print_next     ; Always taken.
0004ECr 3               WOZEXIT:
0004ECr 3  7A                           ply
0004EDr 3  FA                           plx
0004EEr 3  68                           pla
0004EFr 3  28                           plp
0004F0r 3  60                           rts
0004F1r 3               
0004F1r 2               ;
0004F1r 2               ;
0004F1r 2               
0004F1r 1
